. /********************************************************************************
> CONTINUOUS EXPOSURE SENSITIVITY ANALYSIS FOR REVIEWER COMMENT ON MEDIAN SPLITS
> Project: UKB NfL/GFAP, air pollution, metabolome, dementia, PD, mortality
> Purpose:
>     Add sensitivity analyses in which NfL, GFAP, and air-pollution variables
>     are modeled continuously rather than as above/below median indicators.
>
> Main outputs:
>     1) SUPPTABLE_CONTINUOUS_BIOMARKER_AIRPOLLUTION_INTERACTIONS.xlsx
>        Sheet "Continuous_interactions":
>        Weibull transition models with z-scored biomarker x z-scored pollutant
>        interaction terms, for Healthy->PD, Healthy->Dementia, Healthy->Death.
>
>     2) Same workbook, sheet "Spline_tests":
>        Wald tests of nonlinear restricted cubic spline terms for NfL, GFAP,
>        and each air-pollution marker.
>
> Author note:
>     The interaction HR is exp(beta_interaction). Because both terms are z-scored,
>     it represents departure from multiplicativity for a one-SD higher biomarker
>     and one-SD higher pollutant. These estimates are not directly comparable to
>     the median-split HRs in Table 3, but they directly address reviewer concerns
>     about loss of power and possible dose-response obscuring due to dichotomization.
> ********************************************************************************/
.
. use "$DATA\finaldata_unimputedfin_MULTISTATE", clear

.
. ********************************************************************************
. * 0. Define variables and create standardized continuous exposures
. ********************************************************************************
.
. * Biomarkers: use original continuous NPX/log-scale variables from the manuscript.
. local biomarkers "nefl gfap"

.
. * Air-pollution markers: loge-transformed 2010 measures already created upstream.
. local pollutants "lnPM2point5_2010 lnPM10_2010 lnPM2point5_10_2010 lnPM2point5_abs_2010 lnNO2_2010 lnNOX_2010"

.
. * Optional pollution PCs already created upstream.
. capture confirm variable AIRPOLLUTION1

. if !_rc local pollutants "`pollutants' AIRPOLLUTION1"

. capture confirm variable AIRPOLLUTION2

. if !_rc local pollutants "`pollutants' AIRPOLLUTION2"

.
. * Labels for output readability.
. capture label variable nefl "NfL, continuous"

. capture label variable gfap "GFAP, continuous"

. capture label variable lnPM2point5_2010     "PM2.5, 2010, loge"

. capture label variable lnPM10_2010          "PM10, 2010, loge"

. capture label variable lnPM2point5_10_2010  "PM2.5-10, 2010, loge"

. capture label variable lnPM2point5_abs_2010 "PM2.5 absorbance, 2010, loge"

. capture label variable lnNO2_2010           "NO2, 2010, loge"

. capture label variable lnNOX_2010           "NOx, 2010, loge"

. capture label variable AIRPOLLUTION1        "Air-pollution PC1"

. capture label variable AIRPOLLUTION2        "Air-pollution PC2"

.
. * Standardize in the analytic sample to make main effects and interactions comparable.
. foreach v of local biomarkers
  2.     capture confirm variable `v'
  3.     if !_rc
  4.         capture drop z_`v'
  5.         quietly egen z_`v' = std(`v') if sample_final==1
  6.         local vlab : variable label `v'
  7.         if "`vlab'"=="" local vlab "`v'"
  8.         label variable z_`v' "Standardized `vlab'"
  9.
 10.

.
. foreach v of local pollutants
  2.     capture confirm variable `v'
  3.     if !_rc
  4.         capture drop z_`v'
  5.         quietly egen z_`v' = std(`v') if sample_final==1
  6.         local vlab : variable label `v'
  7.         if "`vlab'"=="" local vlab "`v'"
  8.         label variable z_`v' "Standardized `vlab'"
  9.
 10.

.
. * Keep a compact analytic file with standardized continuous variables.
. save "$DATA\finaldata_unimputedfin_MULTISTATE_continuous_sensitivity", replace

file
/opt/notebooks/finaldata_unimputedfin_MULTISTATE_continuous_sensitivity.dta
saved


.
. ********************************************************************************
. * 1. Helper program for Benjamini-Hochberg q-values
. ********************************************************************************
. * This version fixes the earlier error:
. *     q_interaction_all not found
. * by first creating the q-value variable before using [_n-1].
. * It also avoids duplicate-p-value merge problems by merging back on a unique
. * observation id, not on p-values.
.
. capture program drop make_bh_q

. program define make_bh_q
  1.     syntax varname, PVAR(name) QVAR(name)
  2.
.     tempvar obsid rank m rawq
  3.
.     capture drop `qvar'
  4.     gen long `obsid' = _n
  5.
.     preserve
  6.         keep `obsid' `varlist' `pvar'
  7.         keep if !missing(`pvar') & !missing(`varlist')
  8.
.         sort `varlist' `pvar' `obsid'
  9.         by `varlist': gen long `rank' = _n
 10.         by `varlist': gen long `m' = _N
 11.         gen double `rawq' = `pvar' * `m' / `rank'
 12.
.         * Step-up BH monotonicity: work from largest rank to smallest rank.
.         gsort `varlist' -`rank'
 13.         gen double `qvar' = .
 14.         by `varlist': replace `qvar' = `rawq' if _n == 1
 15.         by `varlist': replace `qvar' = min(`rawq', `qvar'[_n-1]) if _n > 1
 16.         replace `qvar' = min(`qvar', 1)
 17.
.         keep `obsid' `qvar'
 18.         tempfile qtmp
 19.         save "`qtmp'", replace
 20.     restore
 21.
.     merge 1:1 `obsid' using "`qtmp'", nogen
 22.     drop `obsid'
 23. end

.
. ********************************************************************************
. * 2. Continuous biomarker x continuous air-pollution interaction models
. ********************************************************************************
.
. tempname H

. tempfile cont_results

. postfile `H' ///
>     str8 biomarker str60 biomarker_label ///
>     str32 pollutant str90 pollutant_label ///
>     byte transition str24 transition_label ///
>     str12 model ///
>     double N events beta_interaction se_interaction HR_interaction LCI_interaction UCI_interaction ///
>     double p_interaction AIC BIC ///
>     using "`cont_results'", replace

(file
/opt/notebooks/ST_95f4_000001.tmp
not found)


.
. forvalues tr = 1/3
  2.
.     local trlabel = cond(`tr'==1, "Healthy->PD", cond(`tr'==2, "Healthy->Dementia", "Healthy->Death"))
  3.
.     foreach b of local biomarkers
  4.         capture confirm variable z_`b'
  5.         if _rc continue
  6.
.         local zb z_`b'
  7.         local blab : variable label `b'
  8.         if "`blab'"=="" local blab "`b'"
  9.
.         foreach p of local pollutants
 10.             capture confirm variable z_`p'
 11.             if _rc continue
 12.
.             local zp z_`p'
 13.             local plab : variable label `p'
 14.             if "`plab'"=="" local plab "`p'"
 15.
.             * Model 1: sociodemographic/SES adjustment, matching manuscript structure.
.             capture noisily streg c.`zb'##c.`zp' SEX AGE NonWhite householdsize SESres ztownsend ///
>                 if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, distribution(weibull) nohr
 16.             if !_rc
 17.                 local intterm c.`zb'#c.`zp'
 18.                 scalar bint = _b[`intterm']
 19.                 scalar seint = _se[`intterm']
 20.                 scalar zint = bint/seint
 21.                 scalar pint = 2*normal(-abs(zint))
 22.                 scalar hrint = exp(bint)
 23.                 scalar lci = exp(bint - invnormal(0.975)*seint)
 24.                 scalar uci = exp(bint + invnormal(0.975)*seint)
 25.                 scalar Nmodel = e(N)
 26.                 capture scalar Kmodel = e(k)
 27.                 if _rc scalar Kmodel = e(rank)
 28.                 scalar AICm = -2*e(ll) + 2*Kmodel
 29.                 scalar BICm = -2*e(ll) + ln(e(N))*Kmodel
 30.                 capture scalar Emodel = e(N_fail)
 31.                 if _rc
 32.                     quietly count if e(sample) & _d==1
 33.                     scalar Emodel = r(N)
 34.
 35.
.                 post `H' ("`b'") ("`blab'") ("`p'") ("`plab'") ///
>                     (`tr') ("`trlabel'") ("Model 1") ///
>                     (Nmodel) (Emodel) (bint) (seint) (hrint) (lci) (uci) (pint) (AICm) (BICm)
 36.
 37.
.             * Model 2: fully adjusted model, matching Table 3.
.             capture noisily streg c.`zb'##c.`zp' zLE8_TOTALSCOREinv SEX AGE NonWhite householdsize SESres ztownsend ///
>                 comorbid srhbr if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, distribution(weibull) nohr
 38.             if !_rc
 39.                 local intterm c.`zb'#c.`zp'
 40.                 scalar bint = _b[`intterm']
 41.                 scalar seint = _se[`intterm']
 42.                 scalar zint = bint/seint
 43.                 scalar pint = 2*normal(-abs(zint))
 44.                 scalar hrint = exp(bint)
 45.                 scalar lci = exp(bint - invnormal(0.975)*seint)
 46.                 scalar uci = exp(bint + invnormal(0.975)*seint)
 47.                 scalar Nmodel = e(N)
 48.                 capture scalar Kmodel = e(k)
 49.                 if _rc scalar Kmodel = e(rank)
 50.                 scalar AICm = -2*e(ll) + 2*Kmodel
 51.                 scalar BICm = -2*e(ll) + ln(e(N))*Kmodel
 52.                 capture scalar Emodel = e(N_fail)
 53.                 if _rc
 54.                     quietly count if e(sample) & _d==1
 55.                     scalar Emodel = r(N)
 56.
 57.
.                 post `H' ("`b'") ("`blab'") ("`p'") ("`plab'") ///
>                     (`tr') ("`trlabel'") ("Model 2") ///
>                     (Nmodel) (Emodel) (bint) (seint) (hrint) (lci) (uci) (pint) (AICm) (BICm)
 58.
 59.
 60.
 61.

. ********************************************************************************
. * 3. Restricted cubic spline sensitivity tests for functional form
. ********************************************************************************
. * These tests evaluate whether nonlinear exposure terms improve the model.
. * They address dose-response/nonlinearity concerns without replacing the main
. * continuous interaction models.
. *
. * IMPORTANT FIX:
. * For restricted cubic splines, Stata mkspline should be called with a stub:
. *     mkspline rcs_ = x, cubic nknots(4)
. * not with a pre-specified list such as:
. *     mkspline spl1 spl2 spl3 spl4 = x, cubic nknots(4)
. * The latter syntax triggers: 'spl2' found where number expected.
. ********************************************************************************
.
. use "$DATA\finaldata_unimputedfin_MULTISTATE_continuous_sensitivity", clear

. capture confirm variable AIRPOLLUTION1

. if !_rc local pollutants "`pollutants' AIRPOLLUTION1"

. capture confirm variable AIRPOLLUTION2

. if !_rc local pollutants "`pollutants' AIRPOLLUTION2"

>     str32 exposure str90 exposure_label ///

>     str12 model ///
>     double N events chi2 df p_nonlin AIC_linear BIC_linear AIC_spline BIC_spline ///
>     using "`spline_results'", replace

. forvalues tr = 1/3
  2.     local trlabel = cond(`tr'==1, "Healthy->PD", cond(`tr'==2, "Healthy->Dementia", "Healthy->Death"))
  3.
.     foreach x of local exposures
  4.         capture confirm variable `x'
  5.         if _rc continue
  6.
.         local xlab : variable label `x'
  7.         if "`xlab'"=="" local xlab "`x'"
  8.
.         /************************************************************************
>         Model 1: linear vs restricted cubic spline functional form
>         ************************************************************************/
.         capture noisily streg c.`x' SEX AGE NonWhite householdsize SESres ztownsend ///
>             if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, distribution(weibull) nohr
  9.
.         if !_rc
 10.             scalar Nmodel = e(N)
 11.             capture scalar Kmodel = e(k)
 12.             if _rc scalar Kmodel = e(rank)
 13.             scalar AICLIN = -2*e(ll) + 2*Kmodel
 14.             scalar BICLIN = -2*e(ll) + ln(e(N))*Kmodel
 15.             capture scalar Emodel = e(N_fail)
 16.             if _rc
 17.                 quietly count if e(sample) & _d==1
 18.                 scalar Emodel = r(N)
 19.
 20.
.             capture drop rcs_*
 21.             capture noisily mkspline rcs_ = `x' if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, cubic nknots(4)
 22.
.             if !_rc
 23.                 unab rcsvars : rcs_*
 24.                 local first_rcs : word 1 of `rcsvars'
 25.                 local nonlinear_rcs : list rcsvars - first_rcs
 26.
.                 capture noisily streg `rcsvars' SEX AGE NonWhite householdsize SESres ztownsend ///
>                     if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, distribution(weibull) nohr
 27.
.                 if !_rc
 28.                     capture scalar Kmodel = e(k)
 29.                     if _rc scalar Kmodel = e(rank)
 30.                     scalar AICSPL = -2*e(ll) + 2*Kmodel
 31.                     scalar BICSPL = -2*e(ll) + ln(e(N))*Kmodel
 32.
.                     * Test only the nonlinear spline components; the first spline
.                     * term is the approximately linear component.
.                     if "`nonlinear_rcs'" != ""
 33.                         capture noisily testparm `nonlinear_rcs'
 34.                         if !_rc
 35.                             post `S' ("`x'") ("`xlab'") (`tr') ("`trlabel'") ("Model 1") ///
>                                 (Nmodel) (Emodel) (r(chi2)) (r(df)) (r(p)) ///
>                                 (AICLIN) (BICLIN) (AICSPL) (BICSPL)
 36.
 37.
 38.                     else
 39.                         post `S' ("`x'") ("`xlab'") (`tr') ("`trlabel'") ("Model 1") ///
>                             (Nmodel) (Emodel) (.) (.) (.) ///
>                             (AICLIN) (BICLIN) (AICSPL) (BICSPL)
 40.
 41.
 42.
 43.
 44.
.         /************************************************************************
>         Model 2: linear vs restricted cubic spline functional form
>         ************************************************************************/
.         capture noisily streg c.`x' zLE8_TOTALSCOREinv SEX AGE NonWhite householdsize SESres ztownsend ///
>             comorbid srhbr if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, distribution(weibull) nohr

. ********************************************************************************
. * 4. Optional margins plots for key continuous PM2.5 interaction models
. ********************************************************************************
. * This produces visual dose-response/effect-modification plots for PM2.5.
. * Comment out this section if figures are not needed.
.
. use "$DATA\finaldata_unimputedfin_MULTISTATE_continuous_sensitivity", clear

.
. foreach b in nefl gfap
  2.     capture confirm variable z_`b'
  3.     if _rc continue
  4.
.     local zb z_`b'
  5.     forvalues tr = 1/3
  6.         local trlabel = cond(`tr'==1, "Healthy to PD", cond(`tr'==2, "Healthy to Dementia", "Healthy to Death"))
  7.         capture noisily streg c.`zb'##c.z_lnPM2point5_2010 zLE8_TOTALSCOREinv SEX AGE NonWhite householdsize SESres ztownsend ///
>             comorbid srhbr if sample_final==1 & _trans`tr'==1, distribution(weibull)
  8.         if !_rc
  9.             capture noisily margins, at(`zb'=(-1 0 1) z_lnPM2point5_2010=(-2 -1 0 1 2)) predict(hazard) post
 10.             if !_rc
 11.                 marginsplot, ///
>                     title("`b' x PM2.5, `trlabel'") ///
>                     ytitle("Predicted hazard") ///
>                     xtitle("PM2.5, z-score") ///
>                     legend(order(1 "`b'=-1 SD" 2 "`b'=mean" 3 "`b'=+1 SD") pos(6) row(1)) ///
>                     name(mp_`b'_tr`tr', replace)
 12.                 graph export "$FIG\CONTINUOUS_`b'_PM25_TRANS`tr'_MARGINSPLOT.tif", replace width(2400)
 13.                 graph save "$FIG\CONTINUOUS_`b'_PM25_TRANS`tr'_MARGINSPLOT.gph", replace
